<link rel='stylesheet' id='wp-pagenavi-css' href='https://www.cuny.edu/wp-content/plugins/wp-pagenavi/pagenavi-css.css?ver=2.70' type='text/css' media='all' />
<link rel='stylesheet' id='js_composer_front-css' href='https://www.cuny.edu/wp-content/plugins/js_composer/assets/css/js_composer.min.css?ver=7.8' type='text/css' media='all' />
<link rel='stylesheet' id='search-filter-plugin-styles-css' href='https://www.cuny.edu/wp-content/plugins/search-filter-pro/public/assets/css/search-filter.min.css?ver=2.5.21' type='text/css' media='all' />
<link rel='stylesheet' id='vc_plugin_table_style_css-css' href='https://www.cuny.edu/wp-content/plugins/easy-tables-vc/assets/css/style.css?ver=1.0.0' type='text/css' media='all' />
<link rel='stylesheet' id='vc_plugin_themes_css-css' href='https://www.cuny.edu/wp-content/plugins/easy-tables-vc/assets/css/themes.css?ver=6.6.2' type='text/css' media='all' />

<style id='cuny_style-inline-css' type='text/css'>
@import url('https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Libre+Franklin:ital,wght@0,200;0,400;0,600;0,700;0,800;1,400;1,900&display=swap');
@import url('https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Instrument+Serif:ital@0;1&display=swap');
@import url("https://use.typekit.net/jzb3bfs.css");
@import url("https://use.typekit.net/mei3cdu.css");
span.vc_tta-title-text {
    hyphens: auto;
    word-break: normal;
}
.bookmania{
font-family: "bookmania", serif;
font-weight: 400;
font-style: normal;
}
.post-list-container img{
    border-radius: 1rem;
    border: 1.5px solid
}
.postid-252688 #spotlight,
.postid-281651 #spotlight {
  display:none;
}
.postid-252688 #news h2.text-heading_large.bold,
.postid-281651 #news h2.text-heading_large.bold {
    font-weight: 600 !important;
}
.border.bg-primary-1 {
    border-color: #000;
}
body #main-logo:focus {
outline: rgb(99, 154, 249) inset 3px !important;
outline-offset: 5px !important;
visibility: visible !important;
opacity: 1 !important;
}

div#main-logo {
    display: block;
}
html {
  scroll-padding-top:100px;
}
/* container */
.two-columns-grid {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr;
}
/* columns */
.two-columns-grid > * {
    padding:1rem;
}

#news h2.text-heading_large{
    font-size: 3.5rem;
    line-height: 1.2;
    font-style: normal;
    font-weight: 800 !important;
    color: #0033a1 !important;
    text-align:center !important;
}
#nominee-breadcrumbs a, #nominee-breadcrumbs, #breadcumbs-blog a{
font-size: .8rem;
color:#000000;
}
#nominee-breadcrumbs a{
    font-weight: 600;
    text-decoration: underline;
}
.single-nominee #page-title{
display:none;
}
.single-nominee h2.color-primary-1.bg-secondary-pearl{
color:#0033a1 !important;
}
.single-nominee #related{
margin-top:64px !important;
}
.single-nominee .border{
border-color:#0033a1;
}
.single-nominee article{
padding-bottom:50px;
}
.single-nominee #main-content{
margin-bottom:0;
}
.single-nominee footer .bg-primary-2{
display:none;
}
.single-nominee p{
 color: #0033a1;
}
.single-nominee .iconlist li {
    display: inline-block;
    color: #0033a1;
    padding-right: 20px;
}
.single-nominee .bg-1 {
    background-size: 300% 300%;
    background-image: linear-gradient(-45deg, rgba(0, 64, 240, 1) 0%, rgba(0, 51, 161, 1) 25%, rgba(0, 64, 240, 1) 51%, rgba(0, 51, 161, 1) 100%);
    animation: AnimateBG 20s ease infinite;
}
.single-nominee #breadcrumbs{
display:none;
}
.promo-logo{
    position: absolute;
    bottom: 0;
    left: 25%;
    right: 25%;
    z-index: 1;
    width: 50% !important;
}
.single-nominee .list-group li {
    position: relative;
    display: inline-block;
    text-decoration: none;
    /* border: 1.5px solid #0033a1; */
    /* padding: .185rem .85rem; */
    padding: .285rem .385rem;
    font-size: 65%;
    line-height: 1;
    text-align: center;
    margin-right: 5px;
    color: #0033a1;
    margin-bottom: 0 !important;
    width: auto;
    border-radius: .25rem;
    backdrop-filter: contrast(.7);
}
@media screen and (max-width: 787px) {
.single-nominee .vc_col-sm-4{
    margin-bottom:0;
    }
}
.mr-10{
  margin-right:10px !important;
}
#post-logo{
max-width:350px;
margin:0 auto;
}
#story{
margin-top:75px;
}
.single #main-content.story{
    margin-bottom:0;
}
.single-news #cuny-page-header #page-title{
    bottom: -0.5%;
}
.single-news #page-title {
display:none;
}
.single-news #breadcrumbs{
display:none;
}
.single-news .entry-meta{
font-size: 1rem;
color:#383838;
}
.single-news .entry-content{
margin-top:50px;
}
.post-title{
font-size:15px;
line-height:1.5;
}
.single-news img.featured {
  margin:0;
border-radius:0;
}
.wp-caption p.wp-caption-text{
padding-top:0;
}
.wp-caption{
  max-width: fit-content;
}
.wp-caption.alignnone{
    margin-right:0;
}
.wp-caption > p:empty {
    display: none;
}
.wp-caption p.wp-caption-text{
    font-family: Georgia, Times, Times New Roman, serif;
    font-size: 14px;
    line-height: 18px;
    padding-top: 0;
    color:#383838;
}
.single .wpb_content_element {
    overflow: inherit;
}
.single .wp-caption{
  display:block;
  background-color:transparent;
}
.single #main-content {
    margin-top: 0;
}
.wp-caption img.size-full,
.single img.size-full{
  margin-left: -100px;
  width: calc(100% + 200px);
  max-width:inherit;
  margin-right: 0;
  margin-bottom: 0;
  margin-top: 0;
}
@media screen and (max-width: 986px){
.wp-caption img.size-full,
.single img.size-full{
  margin-left: 0;
  width: 100%;
  }
}
.single img{
  border-radius: 10px;
  margin: 40px 0px 20px 0;
}
.single .alignleft, img.alignleft {
    display: inline;
    float: left;
    margin-right: 20px;
    margin-top: 0;
}
.wpb_text_column ol{
color:inherit;
}
.slick-next::before {
content: "\e966" !important;
}
.slick-prev::before{
content: "\e965" !important;
}
footer h2 {
font-size: 1.30rem;
line-height: 1.5rem;
}
footer a {
display: inline-block;
min-height: 24px;
min-width: 24px;
}
.twitter-timeline {
display:none !important;
}
.m-0 {
margin: 0 !important;
}
.apply-btn {
padding: 5px 15px;
border-radius: 5px;
display: inline-block;
}
a.bg-auxiliary-1:hover,
a.bg-auxiliary-1:focus {
color: white;
}
#website .ts-font-icon.icon-cuny_logo {
font-size: 80px !important;
}
.wpb_text_column ul, .single-post ul {
margin-bottom: 20px;
}
.cuny-search-form ul {
margin: 0 !important;
}
.nchgrid-caption-text {
padding: 1%;
background-color: #353A3D !important;
}
#skip-links a {
color: #000 !important;  
}
.nch-lightbox-modal.no-ajaxy {
width: 100%;
height: 100%;
display: block;
position: absolute;
top: 0;
left: 0;
}
.nch-lb-main::focus-within {
background: green;
}
#donate a:hover,
#donate a:focus {
color: #989898;
}
#alert-banner a:hover,
#alert-banner a:focus {
color: #0040F0;
font-family: 'Libre Franklin', sans-serif;
font-weight: 600 !important;
}
.paging_simple_numbers a {
display: inline-block;
margin: 12px;
cursor: pointer;
}

/* Start Quote */
blockquote {
padding: 5%;
border: 1px solid #000000;
display: block;
margin-left: 0;
margin-top: 25px;
margin-right: 0 !important;
border-radius: .25rem;
/* overflow: hidden; */
padding: 25px 20px !important;
position: relative;
}
blockquote:before {
top: -13px;
line-height: 46px;
margin-left: 10px;
font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif;
font-weight: 800 !important;
font-style: normal;
display: block;
content: "\201C";
font-size: 80px;
position: absolute;
left: 0;
background: #ffffff;
color: #000000;
}
q, blockquote p {
position: relative;
font-family: 'Libre Franklin', sans-serif;
font-weight: 400 !important;
font-style: normal;
}
blockquote small, blockquote cite #main-content .menu, .widget.widget_categories ul {
font-size: 14px;
line-height: 1.5;
display: inline-block;
padding-top: 15px;
}
/* End of Quote */

ul.contextual-navigation {
list-style-type: none;
border-top: 0 !important;
}
ul.contextual-navigation a {
font-size: .90rem;
text-transform: inherit;
line-height: initial;
letter-spacing: 0;
color: #0040F0;
padding: 10px 5px !important;
}
ul.contextual-navigation a:hover {
background-color: #0040F0 !important;
color:#FFFFFF !important;
}
ul.contextual-navigation.menu li {
padding:0 !important;
margin-bottom:0 !important;
border-bottom: 1px solid #0040F0 !important;
}
ul.contextual-navigation.menu li.current_page_item> a {
background-color: #0040F0 !important;
color:#FFFFFF !important;
}
ul.contextual-navigation .children a {
color: black !important;
}
ul.contextual-navigation .children a:hover {
background-color: transparent !important;
text-decoration:underline !important;
color:inherit;
}
ul.contextual-navigation.menu .children li {
border-bottom: 0 !important;
}
.underline {
display: inline-block;
color: #353a3d;
}
.underline::after {
content: "";
display: block;
background: #FFB81C;
height: 2px;
}
#navbar .vc_column-inner {
padding-top:0;
}
.alert-banner a {
color: #000000;
text-decoration: underline;
display: inline-block;
}
.expandable-target-open {
margin-top: 20px;
}
.mb-0 {
margin-bottom: 0 !important;
}
.broken_link, a.broken_link {
text-decoration: none !important;
}
header .ts-font-icon.icon-cuny_logo,
header .ts-font-icon.icon-cuny_logo:focus,
.ts-font-icon.icon-cuny_logo {
margin-right: 0;
}
form.selectList select, .widget_archive select {
appearance: none;
-webkit-appearance: none;
-moz-appearance: none;
font-size: 17px;
font-weight: normal;
color: #000000;
height: 45px;
padding-right: 5px;

.paging_simple_numbers{

    border-radius: 6px;
    background-color: #0040F0;

    line-height: initial;
    margin-right: 5px;
    font-weight: 500;
}
.selectList,
.paging_simple_numbers{
    font-size: 0.75rem;
    font-weight: 500;
}
.paging_simple_numbers{
    margin-top: 40px;
}
.paging_simple_numbers a {

    padding: 8px 15px;
    border: 1px solid #011d49;

    padding: 8px 15px;

}
 
body, p, ul, ol, td {
font-family: 'Libre Franklin', sans-serif;
font-weight: 400 !important;
font-style: normal;
font-size: 1.15rem;
line-height: 1.71rem;
color: #000000;
word-break: keep-all;
}
td p, td li {
font-size: 0.95rem;
line-height: 1.45rem;
}
.intro, .intro p {
font-family: 'Libre Franklin', sans-serif;
font-weight: 400 !important;
line-height: 36px;
}
/*a {
text-decoration: none,
}*/
a:hover,
a:focus,
a:active {
color: black;
text-decoration: underline;
}
a[href^=mailto:] {
overflow: hidden;
white-space: nowrap;
text-overflow: ellipsis;
}
.wpb_row.full-width .wpb_row,
header .wpb_row .wpb_row,
footer .wpb_row .wpb_row,
.wpb_row {
width: 95%;
}
.cuny-cta-button {
overflow: hidden;
text-align: center;
vertical-align: middle;
border-radius: 0;
padding: 10px 20px !important;
font-family: 'Libre Franklin', sans-serif;
font-weight: 600 !important;
font-size: 1.25rem;
letter-spacing: 0;
line-height: 1;
border: 2px solid #353a3d;
margin: 0 0 35px;
border-radius: 300px;
text-decoration: none;
/*padding: 1rem 2.5rem !important;
text-transform: uppercase;
font-size: .9rem;
line-height: 1.5;*/
}
.tight-container + .cuny-cta-button {
border-radius: 0;
}
.cuny-cta-button a {
width: 100%;
display: block;
color: #353a3d;
}
.btn:hover,
.btn:focus,
a.cuny-cta-button:hover,
a.cuny-cta-button:focus {
background-color: #0040F0;
color: #ffffff !important;
text-decoration: none;
}
.cuny-separator .double {
background-color: #FFB81C;
}
.cuny-separator h1 {
margin-top: 20px;
font-size: 2.45rem;
line-height: 3rem;
color: #1D3A83;
margin-bottom: 0;
text-transform: inherit;
font-weight: normal;
font-family: 'Libre Franklin', sans-serif;
font-weight: 800 !important;
}
/* Formstack Embed Forms */
.fsBody {
  color: #000000 !important;
  font-family: 'Libre Franklin', Arial, Helvetica, sans-serif !important;
  font-size:17px;
  line-height: 17px;
}
.fsBody .fsForm .fsOptionLabel, .fsBody .fsForm .fsLabel {
  margin-bottom:10px;
  color:#000000;
}
.fsBody .fsForm input[type="text"].fsField, 
.fsBody .fsForm input[type="file"].fsField, 
.fsBody .fsForm input[type="number"].fsField, 
.fsBody .fsForm input[type="email"].fsField, 
.fsBody .fsForm input[type="tel"].fsField, 
.fsBody .fsForm textarea.fsField,
.fsBody .fsForm input[type="text"].fsRequired, 
.fsBody .fsForm input[type="file"].fsRequired, 
.fsBody .fsForm input[type="number"].fsRequired, 
.fsBody .fsForm input[type="email"].fsRequired, 
.fsBody .fsForm input[type="tel"].fsRequired, 
.fsBody .fsForm textarea.fsRequired, 
.fsBody .fsForm select, 
.fsBody .fsForm select.fsRequired {
    color: #000000 !important;
    font-family: 'Libre Franklin', Arial, Helvetica, sans-serif;
    font-size: 17px;
    line-height:1.5rem;
    font-weight: 400;
    border: 1px solid #7c7c7c;
    border-radius:4px;
    background-clip: padding-box;
    text-indent: 0;
}
.fsBody .fsForm ::-moz-focus-inner {
  border: none !important;
  outline: 0 !important;
}
.fsBody .fsForm .fsForm select:not([multiple="multiple"]), 
.fsBody .fsForm .fsRowBody input[type="email"], 
.fsBody .fsForm .fsRowBody input[type="number"], 
.fsBody .fsForm .fsRowBody input[type="tel"], 
.fsBody .fsForm .fsRowBody input[type="text"]{
  box-sizing: border-box;
  height: 47px;
}
.fsBody .fsForm .fsSubmitButton {
  padding: 10px 20px !important;
  border-radius: 25px !important;
  text-transform: none !important;
  font-family: 'Libre Franklin', sans-serif;
  font-weight: 600;
  font-size: 1.25rem !important;
  line-height: 1; 
  letter-spacing: normal;
}
.fsBody .fsForm .fsSubmitButton:hover {
    background-color: #4a5053 !important;
}
.fsBody .fsForm .fsSupporting, .fsCounter {
  font-family: 'Libre Franklin', Arial, Helvetica, sans-serif;
  font-size: 12px;
}
.fsBody .fsForm label.fsSupporting {
  padding-bottom: 10px;
  margin-top: 0;
}
.fsBody .fsForm div.fsSupporting {
  margin-top:10px;
}
/* ---- End Formstack Embed Forms ----*/
input[type="text"],
input[type="email"],
input[type="tel"] {
font-size: inherit;
color: inherit;
margin-bottom: 10px;
padding: 10px 0;
width: 100%;
border: 1px solid #000;
text-indent: 10px;
box-sizing: border-box;
border-color: #000000;
border-radius: .25rem;
}
/*Libre Franklin Font*/
.heavy {
font-family: 'Libre Franklin', sans-serif;
font-weight: 800 !important;
font-style: normal;
}
.boldcn {
font-family: 'Libre Franklin', sans-serif;
font-weight: 600 !important;
font-style: normal;
}

.regular {

font-weight: 400 !important;

}
.light {

font-weight: 400 !important;

}

/* End Admissions */
/* -- Table 2023 --  */
table {
border: 0;
border: 0;
font-size: 13px;
line-height: 1.25;
font-size: 13px;
}
th {
border: 0;
border-bottom: 2px solid #000000;
background-color: transparent;
text-transform: uppercase;
vertical-align: bottom;
text-align:center;
font-weight:700 !important;
}
th:first-child {
padding: inherit;
padding-top: 8px;
padding-bottom: 8px;
}
th:focus {
outline: 1px solid black;
}
td {
border: 0;
padding: 8px 15px;
border-bottom: 0.75px solid #000;
font-size: 0.95rem;
line-height: 1.5;
}
td:nth-child(even) {
background-color: #f9f9f9;
}
/* -- End Table 2023 --  */
/*Global Classes*/
.no-margin {
margin-bottom: 0 !important;
margin-top: 0 !important;
}
.wpb_cuny_text_box a {
border: 2px solid transparent;
transition: all 0.3s ease;
}
.wpb_cuny_text_box a:hover, .wpb_cuny_text_box a:focus, .wpb_cuny_text_box a:active {
border-radius: 4px
}
.wpb_cuny_text_box a:hover, .wpb_cuny_text_box a:focus, .wpb_cuny_text_box a:active,
ul#arrow a:hover,
ul#arrow a:focus,

color: #000; 
}

.cuny-search-form .wpb_cuny_text_box a:active,

}
.cuny-search-form .wpb_cuny_text_box a:hover,
.cuny-search-form .wpb_cuny_text_box a:focus {
color: inherit;
}
hr {
  margin-top:25px !important;
}

Divided in primary, secondary, auxiliary and custom fixes === */

/* Color Custom Fixes */
.bg-secondary-slate, .bg-secondary-slate p, .bg-secondary-slate ul, .bg-secondary-slate a,
.bg-secondary-slate h1, .bg-secondary-slate h2, .bg-secondary-slate h3, .bg-secondary-slate h4,

.bg-secondary-umber, .bg-secondary-umber p, .bg-secondary-umber ul, .bg-secondary-umber a,
.bg-secondary-umber h1, .bg-secondary-umber h2, .bg-secondary-umber h3, .bg-secondary-umber h4,
h2.bg-secondary-umber, h3.bg-secondary-umber, h4.bg-secondary-umber,

.bg-auxiliary-strawberry, .bg-auxiliary-strawberry p, .bg-auxiliary-strawberry ul, .bg-auxiliary-strawberry a,

/* New Nav */
.menu-item-190322,
#cuny-hamburger-menu-icon,
#menu-main-navigation-1 li.search,
#menu-main-navigation-1 .menu-item-190897,
#menu-main-navigation-1 .menu-item-190898 {
display: none !important;
}
#cuny-hamburger-menu-overlay {
z-index: 3 !important;
}
#main-quicklinks ul {
text-align: justify;
}
#main-quicklinks ul li {
display: inline-block !important;
text-transform: uppercase;
padding: 0;
font-family: 'Libre Franklin', sans-serif;
font-weight: 600 !important;
text-transform: uppercase;
font-size: 1.25rem; 
}
#main-quicklinks ul li:first-child, #main-quicklinks ul li:last-child {
padding: 0;
}
#cuny-hamburger-menu-overlay #cuny-hamburger-menu-content {
text-align: left !important;
}
#cuny-main-menu ul#menu-main-navigation.menu {
display: block;
line-height: 1px;
}
#cuny-main-menu ul#menu-main-navigation.menu li {
float: left;
width: 100%;
text-align: left;
line-height: 1;
min-height: initial !important;
padding: 15px 0;
}
#cuny-mobile-menu,
#cuny-main-menu ul.sub-menu {
display: none;
}
#cuny-main-menu ul#menu-main-navigation.menu > li,
#cuny-main-menu ul#menu-main-navigation-1.menu > li {
border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(63, 179, 255, 0.2);
position: relative;
display: block;
}
#cuny-main-menu ul#menu-main-navigation.menu li > a,
#cuny-main-menu ul#menu-main-navigation-1.menu li > a {
display: inline-block;
text-align: left;
padding: 0;
}
#cuny-main-menu ul#menu-main-navigation.menu > li,
#cuny-main-menu .mpt-menu-container .menu > li {
font-family: 'Libre Franklin', sans-serif;
font-weight: 600 !important;
font-size: 2rem;
line-height: initial !important;
padding-left: 0;
text-transform: capitalize;
}
#cuny-main-menu ul#menu-main-navigation > li > a {
color: #ffffff;
}
#cuny-main-menu ul#menu-main-navigation > li:hover,
#cuny-main-menu ul#menu-main-navigation > li:hover > a,
#cuny-main-menu ul#menu-main-navigation > li.focus,
#cuny-main-menu ul#menu-main-navigation > li.focus > a {
background-color: transparent;
}
#cuny-main-menu ul#menu-main-navigation > li > a:hover,
#cuny-main-menu ul#menu-main-navigation > li > a.focus {
color: #ffb81c;
}
#menu-main-navigation-1 .sub-menu li {
width: 100%;
margin: 0;
padding: 0;
line-height: 2;
height: initial;
border: 0;
font-size: 1.15rem;
}
.vc_tta-accordion.vc_tta-color-grey .vc_tta-panels .vc_tta-panel-heading a {
background-color: transparent;
}
/* End New Nav */
/* Headings */
h1, h2, h3, h4,
.h2, .h3, .h4 {
text-transform: inherit !important;
font-family: 'Libre Franklin', sans-serif;
font-weight: 800 !important;
font-style: normal;
}
h2 > strong,
h3 > strong,
h4 > strong {
font-family: 'Libre Franklin', sans-serif;
font-weight: 800 !important;
font-style: normal;
}
strong {
font-family: 'Libre Franklin', sans-serif;
font-weight: 700 !important;
font-style: normal;
}
.small,
.small p {
font-size: 0.75rem;
line-height: 1.20rem;
}
.mackinac-pro{
    font-family: p22-mackinac-pro, serif;
    font-weight: 700;
    font-style: normal;
    letter-spacing: -1px;
}
h2,
.h2 {
font-size: 2.25rem;
line-height: 2.65rem;
}
h3,
.h3 {
font-size: 1.65rem;
line-height: 2rem;
}
h4,
.h4 {
font-size: 1.30rem;
line-height: 1.5rem;
}
h1 a::after, h2 a::after, h3 a::after {
content: none;
}
h1, h2, h3, h4 {
text-decoration: none !important;
font-style: normal;
}
h1 a, h2 a, h3 a, h4 a {
color: #0040F0;
font-style: normal;
font-weight: 800;
text-decoration: underline;
}
h2 a:hover, h2 a:active, h2 a:focus,
h3 a:hover, h3 a:active, h3 a:focus,
h4 a:hover, h4 a:active, h4 a:focus {
background-color: #FFB81C !important;
border-bottom: 1px solid #FFB81C;
color: #000000 !important;
text-decoration: underline !important;
-o-transition: .2s;
-ms-transition: .2s;
-moz-transition: .2s;
-webkit-transition: .2s;
transition: .2s;
}
h1.title {
font-family: 'Libre Franklin', sans-serif;
font-weight: 800 !important;
font-size: 7.2em;
line-height: 1;
text-shadow: 1px 1px 2px #606060;
}
h2.cuny-section-header {
font-family: 'Libre Franklin', sans-serif;
font-weight: 600 !important;
font-size: 1.4rem !important;
line-height: 1.8rem !important;
text-transform: uppercase;
}
h3.cuny-section-header {
font-family: 'Libre Franklin', sans-serif;
font-weight: 600 !important;
font-size: 1.2rem;
line-height: 1.5rem;
text-transform: uppercase;
}
.text-heading_large {
font-size: 3.5rem;
line-height: 1.2;
}
.text-heading_small.underline {
text-transform: uppercase !important;
}
.text-heading_medium {
font-size: 2rem;
line-height: 1.3;
} 
.text-heading_small, .vc_figure-caption {
font-size: 1.20rem;
line-height: 1.45rem;
}
/* End Headings */

/* YouTube */
.ts-video-overlay-wrapper .ts-video-overlay-handle {
top: 45%;
}
.ts-video-overlay-wrapper .ts-video-overlay-handle span {
position: initial !important;
width: 60px !important;
height: 60px !important;
bottom: 30px !important;
background-color: rgba(255,184,28,0.8) !important;
border-radius: 50% !important;
background-clip: padding-box;
}
.ts-video-overlay-wrapper .ts-video-overlay-handle span i.handle_click,
.ts-video-overlay-wrapper .ts-video-overlay-handle span i.handle_hover {
background-image: url(https://www.cuny.edu/wp-content/uploads/sites/4/media-assets/play.png) !important;
height: 49px !important;
width: 49px !important;
}
.ts-video-overlay-image:hover,
.ts-video-overlay-image:focus,
.ts-video-overlay-image:active {
box-sizing: border-box;
-moz-box-sizing: border-box;
-webkit-box-sizing: border-box;
border: 2px solid #000000 !important;
}
.ts-video-overlay-image:focus {
outline: 2px solid #0033A1;
}
.bg-primary-1 .ts-video-overlay-image:focus {
outline: 2px solid #FFB71B;
}
/* End YouTube */
/* Utilities */
.pull-right {
float: right !important;
}
.pull-left {
float: left !important;
}
.block {
display: block !important;
}
.inline-block {
display: inline-block !important;
}
.inline-list li {
display: inline list-item !important;
}
/* End Utilities */

color: #0040F0;
}
a.small:hover,
a.small:focus {
font-family: 'Libre Franklin', sans-serif;
font-weight: 600 !important;
}

}

#logo-facebook a:focus i,

/* Make this Website Talk */
#__ba_panel button.textDiv {
font-size: 1.15rem;
line-height: 1.70rem;
color: #0040F0;
}
/* End Make this Website Talk */
/* Newsletter Custom */
.research-header {
    font-size: 2rem;
    line-height: 1;
    font-family: 'Libre Franklin', sans-serif;
    font-weight: 600 !important;
    font-style: normal;
    display: inline-block;
    padding: 20px 20px;
}
/* Newsletter Custom */
/* Memorial Pages */
#memorial h1.text-heading_large {
margin-top: 20px;
font-size: 7rem;
line-height: 1;
letter-spacing: 2rem;
}
/* End Memorial Pages */
/* List with Arrow. Maybe make Sticky Links */
ul#arrow {
list-style-type: none;
}
ul#arrow li:before {
content: "\e82d";
font-family: 'CUNY-Font-Icons' !important;
font-style: normal;
font-weight: normal !important;
display: inline-block;
width: 1em;
margin-left: -1.50em;
padding-right: 10px;
}
ul#arrow li {
line-height: inherit;
}
ul#arrow a {
font-size: .90rem;
text-transform: uppercase;
letter-spacing: .1rem;
text-decoration: none !important;
}
ul#arrow a:hover,
ul#arrow a:focus,
ul#arrow a:active {
color: #000000 !important;
transition: color .15s ease-in-out,background-color .15s ease-in-out,border-color .15s ease-in-out,box-shadow .15s ease-in-out;
text-decoration: underline !important;
}
/* End List with Arrow */
/* Memorial Post */
.memorial-template-default .ts-font-icon.icon-cuny_logo {
color: black !important;
}
.memorial-template-default #alert-banner,
.memorial-template-default #donate,
.memorial-template-default #breadcrumbs,
.memorial-template-default #page-title,
.memorial-template-default footer .bg-primary-2,
.memorial-template-default #fsCell95414091 {
display: none;
}
.memorial-template-default  .wpb_text_column {
margin-bottom: 35px;
}
.memorial-template-default #main-content {
margin: 0;
}
.memorial-template-default .wpb_cuny_text_box h2,
.memorial-template-default .wpb_cuny_text_box h3,
.memorial-template-default .wpb_cuny_text_box h4 {
color: black !important;
}
.msgrid .vc_column-inner .wpb_wrapper {
display: grid;
grid-template-columns: 33% 33% 33%;
}
.msgrid .vc_column-inner .wpb_wrapper .wpb_cuny_text_box .wpb_wrapper {
display: inherit;
}
.msgrid .wpb_wrapper .wpb_text_column {
margin: 10px 15px 15px 15px;
}
/* End Memorial Post */
/* Yellow Shape on Photos */
.bg-right,
.bg-left {
position: relative;
}
.bg-right figure {
position: relative;
padding-right: 15px;
padding-bottom: 15px;
}
.bg-left figure {
position: relative;
padding-bottom: 15px;
padding-left: 15px;
}
.bg-right::before {
content: '';
position: absolute;
width: 50%;
height: 75%;
right: 0;
transition: all 1.5s ease-in-out;
background-color: #FFB81C;
bottom: 0;
}
.bg-left::before {
content: '';
position: absolute;
width: 50%;
height: 75%;
transition: all 1.5s ease-in-out;
background-color: #FFB81C;
bottom: 0;
}
/* End Yellow Shape on Photos */
/* Font Styles */
.thin {
font-weight: 100;
}
.light {
font-weight: 300;
}
.regular {
font-weight: 400;
}
.medium {
font-weight: 500;
}
.bold {
font-weight: 700;
}
.black {
font-weight: 900;
}
#navbar .vc_col-sm-10 {
margin-left: 0 !important;
}
#navbar .vc_col-sm-2 {

}

/* BG-Vid */
#video.vc_row.vc_row-o-full-height {
min-height: 100vh !important;
padding:0;
}
#video .video-container {
position: absolute;
top: 0;
bottom: 0;
width: 100%;
height: 100vh;
overflow: hidden;
}
#video video {
/* Make video to at least 100% wide and tall */
min-width: 100%;
min-height: 100%;
/* Setting width & height to auto prevents the browser from stretching or squishing the video */
width: auto;
height: auto;
/* Center the video */
position: absolute;
top: 50%;
left: 50%;
transform: translate(-50%,-50%);
}
#video .content {
background: rgba(0,0,0,0.45);
position: absolute;
bottom: 0;
left: 0;
right: 0;
width: 100%;
height: 100%;
margin: 0;
box-sizing: border-box;
padding: 20% 0;
line-height: 1.5;
text-align: center;
}

/* Special Bullets */
.check ul,
.ex ul {
margin: 0;
list-style-type: none;
}
.check ul li,
.ex ul li {
position: relative;
padding-left: 25px;
}
.check ul li:before,
.ex ul li:before {
background: #ffb81c;
border-radius: 100%;
border: 1px solid;
margin-right: 10px;
margin-left: -50px;
display: inline-block;
padding: 5px;
width: 28px;
color:initial;
font-size: 20px;
text-align: center;
}
.check ul li:before {
content: "✓";  
}
.ex ul li:before {
content: '✘ ';  
}
.check ul li ul li:before, .ex ul li ul li:before {
  content: none;
}
.check ul li ul, .ex ul li ul {
  list-style-type: disc;
}
.check ul li ul li, .ex ul li ul li {
  padding-left: 0;
  margin-left: -10px;
}
.bubble ol li::marker {
content: '';
}
.bubble ol li::before {
counter-increment: orderedlist;
content: counter(orderedlist);
background-color: #ffb81c;
border-radius: 100%;
border: 1px solid;
margin-right: 10px;
margin-left: -50px;
display: inline-block;
padding: 5px;
width: 28px;
font-size: 20px;
text-align: center;
font-weight: 600 !important;
}
.bubble ol {
counter-reset: orderedlist;
margin: 0;
list-style-type: none;
}
.bubble ol li {
position: relative;
padding-left: 25px;
}
.bubble ol li ul li::marker, .bubble ol li ol li::marker {
content: initial;
}
.bubble ol li ul li:before, .bubble ol li ol li:before {
  content: none;
}
.bubble ol li ul {
  list-style-type: disc;
}
.bubble ol li ol {
  list-style-type: auto;
}
.bubble ol li ul li, .bubble ol li ol li {
  padding-left: 0;
  margin-left: -10px;
}
/* End Special Bullets */

#roles .vc_tta-panel-title a {

line-height: 1;

}

#roles .vc_tta-panel-title > a:active,
#roles .vc_tta-panel-title > a:focus {

}
#roles .vc_tta-controls-icon::before,
#roles .vc_tta-controls-icon::after {

}

right: 0;
}

}
/* End New Nav */

}

scrollbar-width: none;  /* Firefox */
}

}

}

}

#breadcrumbs ul {

#breadcumbs-blog li {

}

#breadcumbs-blog li:after {

}

#breadcumbs-blog li.last:after{

}
/* Global change */
#page-title .wpb_row {
padding: 0;
margin-bottom: 0;
}

#page-title.bg-primary-2 {

/*Portrait Videos*/
#portrait.ts-video-container.ts-video-overlay-true, .ts_video_container.ts_video_overlay_true,
.ts-video-container.ts-video-overlay-true, .ts_video_container.ts_video_overlay_true {

#portrait .ts-video-overlay::before,
.ts-video-overlay::before  {

color: #ffffff;
font-family: "CUNY-Font-Icons";
padding: 10px 6px 10px 14px !important;
font-size: 40px;
line-height: 100%;
display: block;
text-align: center;
position: absolute;
top: 45%;
right: 40%;
margin: 0 auto;
z-index: 2;
-webkit-text-stroke-width: 0.75px;
-webkit-text-stroke-color: black;
background-color: #0040F0;
border-radius: 300px;
border:1px solid #ffffff;
}
#portrait.ts-video-overlay-wrapper,
.ts-video-overlay-wrapper {

}

}

.transparent.vc_tta.vc_tta-style-classic .vc_tta-controls-icon.vc_tta-controls-icon-plus::after {
border-color:white !important;
}
.transparent.vc_tta-style-classic .vc_tta-panel-body {
background-color: transparent !important;
border:0 !important;
}
.transparent.vc_tta-style-classic .vc_active .vc_tta-panel-heading {
border:0 !important;
}
.transparent.vc_tta-style-classic .vc_active {
background: rgba(0, 0, 0, .1) !important;
border-top-left-radius: .25rem !important;
border-top-right-radius: .25rem !important;
}
.vc_tta.vc_tta-accordion.vc_tta-style-classic.transparent a {
color: inherit;
}
.transparent.vc_tta-style-classic .vc_tta-panel-title > a:hover {
border-color:white !important;
background-color: yellow !important;
}
.vc_tta-accordion.vc_tta-style-classic.transparent p {
    color: #ffffff;
}
/* End of Transparent Accordions */

.featured-col-image-holder {
position: relative;
display: -webkit-box;
display: -webkit-flex;
display: -ms-flexbox;
display: flex;
height: 2vw;
padding: 1.1vw 1.5vw;
-webkit-box-pack: center;
-webkit-justify-content: center;
-ms-flex-pack: center;
-webkit-box-align: center;
-webkit-align-items: center;
-ms-flex-align: center;
align-items: center;

border:1px solid #000000;
}
.featured-col-image-holder:hover {

}

  height: 10vw;

  }
}

#programs h3 {
text-align: center;
height: 53px;
display: flex;
vertical-align: middle;
align-items: center;
justify-content: center;
margin-bottom: 0;
line-height: 1.25;
font-size: 0.90rem;
overflow: hidden;
padding: 5px 10px;
}
.no-bullets li {
border-top:1px solid;
line-height:1;
margin-bottom:0 !important;
padding-top:10px;
padding-bottom:10px;
}
.no-bullets li:first-child {
border-top:0;
}
.no-bullets ul{
list-style-type: none; /* Remove bullets */
padding: 0; /* Remove padding */
margin: 0; /* Remove margins */
}
.x-padding{
padding:2.5% 5% !important;
}
.v-img > .vc_column-inner {
/*background-position: 19% !important;*/
background-position: top !important;
}
.rounded .v-img:first-child,
.rounded .h-img:first-child{
    overflow: hidden;
    border-top-left-radius:0.85rem;
    border-bottom-left-radius:0.85rem;
}
.rounded .h-img:nth-child(2),
.rounded .v-img:nth-child(2){
    overflow: hidden;
    border-top-right-radius:0.85rem;
    border-bottom-right-radius:0.85rem;
}
#programs .rounded,
.featured .rounded {
    overflow: hidden;
}
.v-img {
min-height: 850px;
}
.h-img {
min-height: 464px;
}
.no-margin-left .h-img.vc_col-sm-8,
.no-margin-left .v-img.vc_col-sm-8 {

}
.no-margin-left .h-img.vc_col-sm-6,
.no-margin-left .v-img.vc_col-sm-6 {

}

  }

   z-index: 0;

#campaign-nav #menu-programs-nav li#menu-item-217600 a,

/* === CUNY Background Video === */
.cuny-bg-vid > div {
min-height: 75vh;
min-height: 75vh;
width: 100%;
}
.cuny-bg-vid .text-heading_large {
font-size: 4.5rem;
line-height: 5rem;
}
.cuny-bg-vid-wrap video {
position: absolute;
top: 0;
left: 0;
min-width: 100%;
min-height: 100%;
width: 100%;
height: 100%;
z-index: 0;
object-fit: cover;
overflow: revert;
}
.cuny-video-toggle {
right: 1rem;
bottom: 1rem;
padding: 0.5rem;
opacity: .75;
z-index: 2;
position: absolute;
color:transparent;
overflow:hidden;
}
.cuny-video-toggle {
width: 50px;
height: 50px;
border-radius: 300px;
line-height: 50px;
border: 1.95px solid #000000;
z-index: 1;
}
.cuny-video-toggle span {
white-space: nowrap;
overflow: hidden;
text-align: left;
}
.cuny-video-toggle .cuny-bgvid-play::before {
content: "\e952";
/*content: "\f0da";*/
font-size: 18px;
}
.cuny-video-toggle .cuny-bgvid-pause::before {
content: "\e94d";
/*content: "\f04c";*/
font-size: 18px;
}
.cuny-video-toggle .cuny-bgvid-play::before,
.cuny-video-toggle .cuny-bgvid-pause::before {
color: #fffcd5;
font-family: "CUNY-Font-Icons";
/*font-family: "ts-fontawesome";*/
width: 32px;
height: 32px;
line-height:32px;
display: block;
text-align: center;
margin: 0 auto;
text-shadow: 1px 1px 0px black;
-webkit-text-stroke-width: 1px;
-webkit-text-stroke-color: black;
}
/* === END CUNY Background Video === */

/* === Jump to Links === */
ul#list-group-pill, ul.list-group-pill {
margin: 0;
padding: 0;
list-style-type: none;
text-align: center;
list-style-position: inside;
-webkit-columns: 1;
-moz-columns: 1;
columns: 1;
}
#list-group-pill li, .list-group-pill li {
border: 0;
padding: 0;
width: 100%;
display: inline-grid;
}
#list-group-pill li a, .list-group-pill li a {
border: 2px solid;
line-height: initial;
font-size: 1rem;
border-radius: 300px;
padding: 10px 15px;
font-weight: 700;
border-color: #0040F0;
color: #0040F0;
align-self: center;
text-decoration: none;
}
.col-2 ul#list-group-pill, .col-2 ul.list-group-pill {
padding: 0;
list-style-type: none;
text-align: center;
list-style-position: inside;
display: grid;
grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr;
column-gap: 1em;
}
.col-2 #list-group-pill li a, .col-2 .list-group-pill li a {
font-weight: 600;
color: #0040F0;
}
.col-3 ul#list-group-pill, .col-3 ul.list-group-pill {
display: grid;
grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr 1fr;
column-gap: 1em;
}
.col-4 ul#list-group-pill, .col-4 ul.list-group-pill {
display: grid;
grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr 1fr 1fr;
column-gap: 1em;
}
#list-group-pill li a:hover,
#list-group-pill li a:focus,
.col-2 #list-group-pill li a:hover,
.col-2 #list-group-pill li a:active,
.col-2 #list-group-pill li a:focus,
.list-group-pill li a:hover,
.list-group-pill li a:focus,
.col-2 .list-group-pill li a:hover,
.col-2 .list-group-pill li a:active,
.col-2 .list-group-pill li a:focus {
background-color: #0040F0 !important;
color: #ffffff !important;
text-decoration: none;
}

@media screen and (max-width: 787px){
  ul#list-group-pill,
  .col-3 ul#list-group-pill,
    ul.list-group-pill,
  .col-3 ul.list-group-pill {
  -webkit-columns: 1;
  -moz-columns: 1;
  columns: 1;
  /*display: inline-block;*/
  }
  .col-3 ul#list-group-pill, .col-3 ul.list-group-pill,
  .col-2 ul#list-group-pill, .col-2 ul.list-group-pill {
  grid-template-columns: 1fr;
  }
}

/*** Font Icon List ***/
.iconlist li:before {
font-family: 'CUNY-Font-Icons';
display: inline-block;
margin-left: calc( var(--icon-space) * -1 );
width: var(--icon-space);
font-size: 26px;
vertical-align: bottom;
}

.iconlist {
margin-top: 15px !important;
margin-left: 15px !important;
margin-right: 5px !important;
--icon-space: 1.2em;
list-style: none;
}
.iconlist li {
padding-left: var(--icon-space);

padding-bottom: var(--icon-space);
margin-bottom: 0 !important;
border: none;
min-height: unset;
display: block;
font-size: 0.975rem;
/*padding-right: 5px;*/
margin-right: 20px;
}
.iconlist li a {
  overflow-wrap: break-word !important;
}
.iconlist .hidden {
display: none;
}
.iconlist li.business:before {
content: "\e909";
}
.iconlist li.calculator:before {
content: "\e90a";
}
.iconlist li.calendar:before {
content: "\e90b";
}
.iconlist li.campus:before {
content: "\e90c";
}
.iconlist li.download:before {
content: "\e919";
}
.iconlist li.email:before {
content: "\e91b";
}
.iconlist li.gear:before {
content: "\e923";
}
.iconlist li.handshake:before {
content: "\e927";
}
.iconlist li.highlights:before {
content: "\e92a";
}
.iconlist li.home:before {
content: "\e92b";
}
.iconlist li.hours:before {
content: "\e962";
}
.iconlist li.housing:before {
content: "\e92c";
}
.iconlist li.instagram:before {
content: "\e931";
}
.iconlist li.laptop:before,
.iconlist li.join:before {
content: "\e935";
}
.iconlist li.linkedin:before {
content: "\e93b";
}
.iconlist li.list:before {
content: "\e93c";
}
.iconlist li.location:before {
content: "\e93f";
}
.iconlist li.nearby:before {
content: "\e948";
}
.iconlist li.mascot:before {
content: "\e941";
}
.iconlist li.money:before {
content: "\e92e";
}
.iconlist li.network:before {
content: "\e949";
}
.iconlist li.phone:before {
content: "\e950";
}
.iconlist li.programs:before {
content: "\e954";
}
.iconlist li.students:before,
.iconlist li.profile:before {
content: "\e963";
}
.iconlist li.transportation:before {
content: "\e96d";
}
.iconlist li.usa:before {
content: "\e971";
}
.iconlist li.vtour:before {
content: "\e972";
}
.iconlist li.website:before {

}

}

/*** External Links Icon ***/
a[target=_blank]:after {
/*content: "\e91d";
margin-left: 10px;
font-family: 'CUNY-Font-Icons' !important;
margin-left: 3px;
line-height: normal;
font-size: initial;*/
    margin-left: 5px;
    line-height: normal;
    font-size: initial;
    content: "";
    display: inline-block;
    width: 6px;
    height: 6px;
    background-image: url(data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20width%3D%2214%22%20height%3D%2214%22%20viewBox%3D%220%200%2014%2014%22%20xmlns%3D%22http%3A//www.w3.org/2000/svg%22%3E%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M10.8101%201.96222L0.726954%2012.0453L1.66171%2012.9801L11.7448%202.89698L11.9344%209.4447L13.208%209.07311L13.0134%202.35278C12.9877%201.46249%2012.2434%200.718185%2011.3531%200.692412L4.80762%200.502924L4.43487%201.77539L10.8101%201.96222Z%22%20fill%3D%22white%22%20stroke%3D%22white%22%20stroke-width%3D%220.542084%22/%3E%3C/svg%3E);
    background-size: contain;
    background-repeat: no-repeat;
    margin-right: 0.5em;
    background-color: #0040F0;
    border-radius: 5px;
    background-origin: border-box;
    background-origin: padding-box;
    background-origin: content-box;
    padding: 5px;
    vertical-align:initial;
}
a.ts-font-icons-link[target=_blank]:after,
a.vc_single_image-wrapper[target=_blank]:after{
display:none;
background-image:none;
}
/*** List ***/
#list-group, .list-group {
    margin: 0;
    padding: 0;
    list-style-type: none;
    text-align: center;
}
#list-group li, .list-group li {
    border: 0;
    padding: 0;
    width: 100%;
    display: inline-grid;
}
#list-group.badge li a, .list-group.badge li a{
    line-height: initial;
    text-decoration: none;
    align-self: center;
    border: 1px solid;
    font-size: 1rem;
    border-radius: .25rem;
    padding: 18px 15px;
    font-weight: 600;
}
#list-group.pill li a, .list-group.pill li a {
    line-height: initial;
    text-decoration: none;
    align-self: center;
    border: 2px solid;
    font-size: 1rem;
    border-radius: 300px;
    padding: 10px 15px;
    font-weight: 700;
}
#list-group.col-4, .list-group.col-4 {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr 1fr 1fr;
  column-gap: 1em;
}
#list-group.col-3, .list-group.col-3 {
    display: grid;
    column-gap: 1em;
    grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr 1fr;
}
#list-group.col-2, .list-group.col-2 {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr;
    column-gap: 1em;
}
@media screen and (max-width: 787px){
#list-group.col-4, #list-group.col-3, #list-group.col-2{
    display: block;
    }
}
/*** End of Links ***/
a.cuny-cta-button[href$=".doc"]::after,
a.cuny-cta-button[href$=".docx"]::after,
a.cuny-cta-button[href$=".pdf"]::after,

a.cuny-cta-button[href$=".pptx"]::after,
a.cuny-cta-button[href$=".xls"]::after,
a.cuny-cta-button[href$=".xlsx"]::after,
a.cuny-cta-button[href$=".csv"]::after,
a.cuny-cta-button[href$=".rtf"]::after,
a.cuny-cta-button[href$=".xls"]::after {
    /*background-color: transparent;*/
    color: inherit;
}

}
/* News - University */
.news-post-title{
   border: 1px solid;
    border-radius: 1rem;
    border-color: inherit;
    padding: 20px 40px;
    font-size: 2rem;
    line-height: 1;
    font-family: 'Libre Franklin', sans-serif;
    font-weight: 600 !important;
    font-style: normal;
    display: inline-block;
    margin-bottom: 0;
}
#container-img {
    margin-top: 40px;
    margin-bottom: 40px;
}
#news p:empty{
    display:none;
}
#news .relatedposts{
    padding-top:64px;
    padding-bottom:64px;
}
#news .relatedposts h4 a{
    font-family: 'Libre Franklin', sans-serif;
    font-weight: 600 !important;
    text-decoration: none !important;
}
#news .entry-meta{
    font-size: 1rem;
    color:#383838;
}
#news .post-title,
h2.post-title.bold{
    font-size: 2.5625rem;
    line-height: 2.8125rem;
    font-family: 'Libre Franklin', sans-serif;
    font-weight: 600 !important;
    font-style: normal;
    text-transform: inherit;
}
#news h2, #news h2 b, #news h2 b i {
    font-size: 1.5625rem;
    line-height: 1.8125rem;
    font-style: italic;
    font-weight: 100 !important;
    margin-bottom:20px;
    text-align:left !important;
/*    text-transform: inherit !important;*/
}
#news h2:last-child {
    margin-bottom:0;
}
#news img.featured {
    margin:0;
    border-radius:0;
}
#news .wp-caption p.wp-caption-text{
    padding:0;
}
#news .wp-caption{
    max-width: fit-content;
}
#news .wp-caption.alignnone{
    margin-right:0;
}
#news .wp-caption > p:empty {
    display: none;
}
#news .wp-caption p.wp-caption-text{
    font-size: 0.9375rem;
    line-height: 1.25rem;
    color: #666F74;
    font-family:inherit;
}
#news .wpb_content_element {
    overflow: inherit;
}
#news .wp-caption{
    display:block;
    background-color:transparent;
    width:100% !important;
}
#news #main-content {
    margin-top: 0;
}
#news .wp-caption img.size-full,
#news img.size-full,
#news img.alignright,
#news img.alignleft,
#news iframe,
#news .two-columns-grid,
.blogs-template-default #main-content iframe{
    margin-left: -100px;
    width: calc(100% + 200px);
    max-width:inherit;
    margin-right: 0;
    margin-bottom: 0;
    margin-top: 0;
    float:none;
}
#news img{
    border-radius:10px;
}
#news img.alignright,
#news img.aliggnleft{
    float:none;

}
#news iframe,
.blogs-template-default #main-content iframe{
    height: 35vw;
    min-height: 35vw;
    border-radius: 10px;
}
#spotlight{
    padding-left:100px;
}
#spotlight img{
    margin: 0;
}
#spotlight a{
    font-size: 15px;
}
#cuny-related .wpb_text_column{
    margin-top: 35px;
}
/* Share this */
#st-2 img{
    margin:0;
    border-radius:0;
}
#st-2 .st-btn{
    border-color: #000000 !important;    
}
.two-columns-grid img{
padding:0 !important;
margin-top:0;
}
@media screen and (max-width: 986px){

jQuery( document ).ready( function($) {

// When the user scrolls down 20px from the top of the document, slide down the navbar
// When the user scrolls to the top of the page, slide up the navbar (50px out of the top view)
window.onscroll = function() {scrollFunction()};


function scrollFunction() {
  if (document.body.scrollTop > 20 || document.documentElement.scrollTop > 20) {
    document.getElementById("sticky-navbar").style.top = "0";
    if($('#cuny-navbar-toggle').hasClass('open')) {

        cuny_vc_main_menu_overlay($, $('#cuny-navbar-toggle' ), null);

  } else {

        console.log('hello');

  }
}
});

            <div class='wpb_cuny_text_box wpb_text_column wpb_content_element box-white'>
                <div class='wpb_wrapper'><p>The CUNY Office of Communications and Marketing works in partnership with the University’s academic and administrative offices and the 26 colleges to promote CUNY’s unique mission and transformative role as an engine of social mobility. The office includes eight divisions: <a href="#communications"><strong>Communications</strong></a>, <a href="#creative"><strong>Creative</strong></a>, <strong><a href="#marketing">Marketing</a>, <a href="#social-media">Social Media</a>, <a href="#multimedia">Multimedia</a></strong>, <a href="#events"><strong>University Events</strong></a>, <strong><a href="#citizenshipnow">Citizenship Now!</a></strong> and <strong><a href="#cunytv">CUNY TV</a></strong>.</p>
<p>For press inquiries, please email <a href="mailto:mediarelations@cuny.edu">mediarelations@cuny.edu</a>. For non-press inquiries, email <a href="mailto:CommsMarketingOffice@cuny.edu" rel="noopener noreferrer">CommsMarketingOffice@cuny.edu</a>.</p>
 </div>

205 East 42nd St.<br />
New York, NY 10017</p>

<script>
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
  const container = document.querySelector("#nominee-2025");
  if (!container) return;

  container.querySelectorAll(".slide img, .modal-content img").forEach(img => {
    if (img.alt && !img.nextElementSibling?.classList.contains("img-caption")) {
      const caption = document.createElement("div");
      caption.className = "img-caption";
      caption.textContent = img.alt;
      img.insertAdjacentElement("afterend", caption);
    }
  });
});
</script>

<!-- // Fix Owl/TS carousel buttons for ADA: remove role="presentation" and add aria-label for screen reader -->
<script>
(function() {
  function fixButton(btn) {
    // Only run if not already fixed
    if (!btn.hasAttribute('aria-label')) {
      btn.removeAttribute('role'); // remove presentation
      const label = btn.classList.contains('owl-prev') ? 'Previous slide' : 'Next slide';
      btn.setAttribute('aria-label', label);
      const span = btn.querySelector('span');
      if (span) span.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
    }
  }

  function fixAllButtons() {
    document.querySelectorAll('.owl-nav .owl-prev, .owl-nav .owl-next').forEach(fixButton);
  }

  // Run after DOM ready
  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', fixAllButtons);
  } else {
    fixAllButtons();
  }

  // Watch for dynamically created/updated buttons
  const navContainers = document.querySelectorAll('.owl-nav');
  if (navContainers.length > 0) {
    const observer = new MutationObserver(fixAllButtons);
    navContainers.forEach(nav => observer.observe(nav, { childList: true, subtree: true }));
  }

  // Fallback: periodically check every 500ms for new navs (covers very dynamic sliders)
  setInterval(fixAllButtons, 500);
})();
</script>


<script>
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
  // Select all TS Video / YouTube iframes
  document.querySelectorAll('iframe[src*="youtube.com/embed"]').forEach(function(iframe, index) {
    
    // Only set title if it doesn't exist
    if (!iframe.hasAttribute('title') || iframe.getAttribute('title').trim() === '') {
      // You can customize the text here; index helps avoid duplicate titles
      iframe.setAttribute('title', 'Video content from YouTube, video ' + (index + 1));
    }
    
    // Optional: ensure the iframe is focusable
    if (!iframe.hasAttribute('tabindex')) {
      iframe.setAttribute('tabindex', '0');
    }
    
  });
});
</script>


<!-- // Accessibility Fix: Make Owl Carousel scrollable region and YouTube images keyboard accessible -->

<script>
// Requires jQuery to be loaded before this script
(function ($) {
  'use strict';

  /**
   * Set tabindex="0" on <img> elements within the given jQuery collection.
   * @param {jQuery} $roots
   */
  function setTabindexOnImages($roots) {
    if (!$roots || !$roots.length) return;

    $roots.each(function () {
      const el = this;

      // If the root itself is an <img>, set tabindex
      if (el.tagName && el.tagName.toLowerCase() === 'img') {
        $(el).attr('tabindex', '0');
      }

      // Set tabindex on all descendant <img> elements
      $(el).find('img').attr('tabindex', '0');
    });
  }

  /**
   * Observe a container and set tabindex="0" on any <img> nodes added under it.
   * @param {Element} container - a DOM element (not jQuery-wrapped)
   */
  function observeNewImages(container) {
    if (!container || typeof MutationObserver === 'undefined') return;

    const observer = new MutationObserver(function (mutations) {
      mutations.forEach(function (mutation) {
        mutation.addedNodes.forEach(function (node) {
          if (node.nodeType !== 1) return; // only element nodes

          // If the added node is an <img>
          if (node.tagName && node.tagName.toLowerCase() === 'img') {
            $(node).attr('tabindex', '0');
          }

          // If a subtree was added, find images inside it
          $(node).find && $(node).find('img').attr('tabindex', '0');
        });
      });
    });

    observer.observe(container, {
      childList: true,
      subtree: true
    });
  }

  function init() {
    // 1) Set tabindex on existing images inside both scopes
    const $anysliderContainers = $('.ts-vcsc-anyslider-container');
    const $videoOverlayWrappers = $('.ts-video-overlay-wrapper');

    setTabindexOnImages($anysliderContainers);
    setTabindexOnImages($videoOverlayWrappers);

    // 2) Observe only the .ts-vcsc-anyslider-container for future images
    $anysliderContainers.each(function () {
      observeNewImages(this);
    });

    // OPTIONAL: If .ts-video-overlay-wrapper content is also dynamically injected,
    // uncomment the block below to observe those as well.
    /*
    $videoOverlayWrappers.each(function () {
      observeNewImages(this);
    });
    */
  }

  // Run after DOM is ready
  $(init);

})(jQuery);
</script></body>
